Projection onto nonnegative sum S:
Function simplex_project(z, S): Need find λ such that sum max(z-λ,0)=S. Since z maybe large. Use binary search between min(z) and max(z). Function monotone. Implement simplex_project(v, s):
  v = np.asarray(v)
  mu = np.sort(v)[::-1]
  cv = np.cumsum(mu)
  r = np.arange(1, len(v)+1)
  cond = mu - (cv - s)/r > 0
  idx = np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]
  theta = (cv[idx] - s)/(idx+1)
  return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
This is O(n log n) in C, good. But repeated line search sorting each candidate expensive. We can project z candidates using same sorted? z = a - t d. d fixed, sorting changes with t. Could use binary with few iterations? Maybe Numpy vectorized 30*7000 per candidate, if 10 candidates*1000=210M ok. Sorting 10*1000*7000log ~? Might be okay. Let's estimate: sorting 7000 in numpy maybe 0.2ms; 10k sorts=2s? Maybe acceptable. 2400s plenty. But Python overhead maybe 1ms. Still okay. We can limit iterations 5000.

But for projections along line, we can use Euclidean projection onto simplex via sorting each time. Good.

def multiplicative_refine(a0, max_wall_s, rng, allow_report=False, global_best? Since no closures, can't pass mutable nonlocal? We can return. To update global, caller does.
   a = normalize(a0,S)
   loga = log(max(a, min))
   S = sqrt(2n)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a); best_loga=loga.copy()
   t_start=monotonic()
   # Define configs list maybe based on n.
   configs = build_weight_configs(n)
   cfg_idx=0
   no_improve=0
   while time < end:
      c, M, sc = conv_score
      if sc<best_sc: update.
      # If no improve many, perturb best and reset? But inside use current a.
      for attempt maybe ?
      # Choose config.
      w=make_config(...)
      h=weighted_cross(a,w)
      mu=dot(a,h)/S
      d=mu-h
      sigma=sqrt(mean(d*d)) + eps
      # Try eta grid maybe [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.6]. Choose based on history? Use grid.
      best_trial = None
      for eta in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.6]:

      else:
          no_improve++
          # try small random multiplicative step? Or accept small worsening to move? In strict refine, move to best_trial even if not improve? If we move to trial with small worsening, could escape. Let's allow mini simulated annealing:
          if no_improve > 30:

          else cfg_idx=(cfg_idx+1)%len(configs)

LSE objective:
def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
 c=fast_conv(a); S=sum; if S small; cmax=max; scaled=beta*(c-cmax); exp; p=exp/sum; L=cmax+logsum/beta; score=2nL/S^2; g=2*cross_derivative weights p; grad=2n(g/S^2 - 2L a/S^3). Return.
This uses all constraints. For high beta (1000), p near max. Good.

Let's design exact FFT functions robust:
FFT_SIZE_CACHE = {}
def get_fft_size(length):
    key=length
    if key not in FFT_SIZE_CACHE:
       # need next pow2 >= length
       size=1
       while size < length: size <<=1
       FFT_SIZE_CACHE[key]=size
    return FFT_SIZE_CACHE[key]

def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    M=2*n-1
    N=get_fft_size(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    return c[:M]

def fast_cross(a,b):
    na=a.size; nb=b.size
    M=na+nb-1
    N=get_fft_size(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)[:M]

def weighted_lag_gradient(a,w):
    n=a.size
    rev=a[::-1]
    conv=fast_cross(rev,w)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]

Check slice: conv length 3n-2; n-1 to 2n-1 inclusive? Python slice n-1:2*n-1 length n. Good.

def score_and_conv(a):

Log init:
def make_loga(a):
   return np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15))

def loga_to_a(loga,S):
   lt=loga - np.max(loga)
   a=np.exp(lt)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return default
   a*=S/s
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-15)
   a*=S/a.sum()
   return a

def weights_top(c, frac, eps=1e-4):
   L=c.size
   k=max(1,int(round(L*frac)))
   k=min(k,L)
   if k==L:
     w=np.ones(L)/L
   else:
     # np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:] if k < L, if k=0 no.
     if k == L: ...
     idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
     w=np.zeros(L)
     w[idx]=1.0
     w/=w.sum()
   if eps>0:
     L=len(c); w=(1-eps)*w + eps/L
   w= np.where(w>0,w,0)
   return w

But argpartition with -k when k=L raises? Handle.

def weights_threshold(c, M, delta, power=1, eps=1e-5):
   L=c.size
   if M <= 1e-30:
      w=np.ones(L)/L
   else:
      thresh = M * max(0.0,1.0-delta)
      pos = np.maximum(c - thresh, 0.0)
      ps = pos.sum()
      if ps <= 1e-30:
         # if only max equals? include max
         w=np.zeros(L); w[np.argmax(c)] =1.0
      else:
         w = pos.copy()
         if power !=1:
            w = np.power(w, power)
         if w.sum() <=1e-30: w=np.ones(L)
   if eps>0: w = (1-eps)*w/w.sum() + eps/L
   return w

If c has numerical negative small, pos. For delta=0, thresh=M, pos only max (maybe multiple) not zero if exact equal; if unique M, pos=0 except max 0? max-thresh=0, ps=0. Need include max. Use pos = np.maximum(c - (M*(1-delta)), 0). If delta=0, pos all 0. We can if ps<=eps set top1(s). In scheduler avoid delta=0 or set small.

def weights_softmax(c, M, beta, eps=1e-5):
   L=c.size
   if M<=...:
     w=ones/L
   else:
     scaled = beta*(c-M)/M
     # max=0
     w=np.exp(np.minimum(scaled,0.0))
     s=w.sum(); if s<=0: w=np.zeros; w[argmax]=1
     else w/=s
   if eps>0: w=(1-eps)*w + eps/L

def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, seed=12345, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t0 + max(60, float(budget_s)-20)
    parent = load_parent()
    candidates=[]
    best_score=inf; best_a=None
    function add_candidate(a,label): normalize, score, update
    lengths = ...
    for n in lengths:
       a = make_initial(n, parent, rng)
       add_candidate(a,label)
    # Also add coarsened-upsampled? later.
    # Sort candidates by score (needs stored). Use list tuples.
    # Main refine best
    sc,a,loga = multiplicative_refine(a, deadline-t0*0.6? etc, rng)
    add_candidate(a,'primary')
    # L-BFGS polish if time
    if time < deadline-60: parse run_lbfgs for betas list, time chunk
    # Explore other candidates:
    for cand in others (up to 3) while time:
       alloc = min(120, (deadline - now)*0.2?) 
       sc,a,loga = multiplicative_refine(cand.a, alloc, rng)
       add_candidate
    # coarse refinement
    if time: m=...; a_m=downsample(best_a,m); refine m small; a=upsample to len(best); refine len moderate; add.
    # final refine best with remaining-30
    sc,a,loga = multiplicative_refine(best_a, deadline - now - 25, rng)
    add.
    # final L-BFGS with remaining
    if time < deadline-10: run lbfgs best
    # final multipulative tiny
    # output best_a scaled
```

def build_weight_configs(rng):
    configs=[]
    for frac in [0.0005,0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35]: configs.append(('top',frac))
    for delta in [0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]:
       configs.append(('thr',delta,1))
       if delta<=0.1: configs.append(('thr',delta,2))
    for beta in [0.5,1,2,5,10,20,50,100,300,1000]: configs.append(('soft',beta))
    rng.shuffle(configs)
    # put a few balanced first? 
    return configs

def run_lbfgs(a0,beta,maxiter,maxfun):
    global LSE_BETA
    LSE_BETA=float(beta)
    a0=np.clip(a0,0,1000).astype(np.float64).copy()

    n=a0.size
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    res=minimize(lbfgs_lse_fun, a0, jac=lbfgs_lse_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={...})
    a_out=np.clip(res.x,0,1000)
    if a_out.sum()<1e-6: a_out=a0
    sc=exact_score(a_out)
    return sc,a_out

```
def multiplicative_refine(a0, max_wall_s, rng, tag=''):
    a0 = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(a0,float)).copy()
    if a0.size<2: return inf ...
    n=a0.size
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_sum(a0,S)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-15)
    loga=make_loga(a)
    sc,c,M = score_and_conv(a)
    best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); best_loga=loga.copy()
    cur_a=a; cur_loga=loga.copy(); cur_sc=sc
    configs=build_weight_configs(rng)
    cfg_idx=0
    no_improve=0
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    end=t_start+max(1.0, max_wall_s)
    print start
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       # if cur_sc is inf reset
       if not np.isfinite(cur_sc): cur_a=best_a; cur_loga=best_loga; cur_sc=best_sc
       sc,c,M=score_and_conv(cur_a)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=cur_a.copy(); best_loga=cur_loga.copy(); print
       # if best a better than cur by much? maybe reset cur to best occasionally to improve
       if cur_sc > best_sc + 1e-4:
           cur_a=best_a; cur_loga=best_loga.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
       accepted=False
       for _ in range(len(configs)):
           cfg=configs[cfg_idx % len(configs)]; cfg_idx +=1
           if time>end: break
           w=get_weights(c,M,cfg)
           h=weighted_lag(cur_a,w)
           # if h all zero? skip
           mu=float(np.dot(cur_a,h)/S)
           d=mu-h
           sigma=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)))
           if sigma <=1e-15:
              continue
           d=np.clip(d/sigma, -5.0,5.0)
           best_trial_sc=cur_sc; best_trial_a=cur_a; best_trial_loga=cur_loga.copy()
           # maybe try eta grid; if cur_sc already best, still.
           for eta in ETA_GRID:
              logt=cur_loga + eta*d
              # recenter/clip to avoid drift
              logt = np.clip(logt - np.max(logt), -60.0, 60.0)
              at=loga_to_a(logt,S)
              if at.size != n or at.sum()<=1e-12: continue
              st=exact_score(at)
              if st < best_trial_sc:
                  best_trial_sc=st; best_trial_a=at; best_trial_loga=logt.copy()
           if best_trial_sc < cur_sc - 1e-14:
              cur_a=best_trial_a; cur_loga=best_trial_loga.copy(); cur_sc=best_trial_sc; accepted=True; no_improve=0
              break
           # if best_trial_sc < cur_sc + 1e-6 maybe accept tiny
           elif best_trial_sc < cur_sc + 1e-10:
              # accept to break plateau
              cur_a=best_trial_a; cur_loga=...; cur_sc=best_trial_sc; accepted=True; no_improve=0
              break
       if not accepted:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve >= 25:
               # perturbation from best

```
def smooth_perturb(a, rng, amp):
    n=a.size; x=np.linspace(0,1,n); f=1.0+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*(1+rng.random()*8)*x+ rng.random()*2*np.pi)
    a2=a*f
    # optional normalize with small smoothing
    a2 = np.convolve(a2, np.array([1,2,3,2,1],dtype=float)/9.0, mode='same')
    a2=np.maximum(a2,1e-15); return normalize_sum(a2,a.sum())
```

def make_initial_sequence(n, parent, rng):
   if parent is not None:
      if parent.size == n: a=parent.copy()
      else: a=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,parent.size), parent)
   else:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      t1=np.power(x+1e-3, -0.45) * np.exp(-0.2*x)
      t2=np.power(np.sin(0.5*np.pi*x), 1.5)
      t3=np.power(np.cos(0.5*np.pi*x), 1.5)
      t4=0.5 + 0.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*(2+rng.random())*x + rng.random()*6.28)
      a=0.35*t1+0.2*t2+0.2*t3+0.25*np.maximum(t4,0)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-6)
   # small log noise
   if n>50:
      a *= np.exp(0.02*rng.standard_normal(n))
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-8)
   return normalize_sum(a, np.sqrt(2*n))
```

Let's refine `lse_a_grad` code:
```
def lse_a_grad(a, beta):
    n=a.size
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12:
       return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    c=fast_conv(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    scaled = beta*(c - cmax)
    scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if es <= 1e-300:
       p = np.zeros_like(c); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else:
       p = e/es
    L = cmax + float(np.log(es))/beta
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    h = weighted_lag_gradient(a,p)
    score = 2.0*n*L/S2
    grad_a = 4.0*n*(h/S2 - L*a/S3)
    # ensure finite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_a)): grad_a.fill(0); score=1e10
    return score, grad_a
```
Is `np.log(es)` safe if es<1? es>=1 due max exp0, so log<=0? Actually if max scaled=0, es>=1, log nonnegative. L>=cmax? Wait LSE should be >=cmax: cmax + logsum/beta, logsum>=0, yes. Good.

  if not finite: zeros
  a=loga_to_a(x,LBFGS_LOG_S)
  sc,ga=lse_a_grad(a,LSE_BETA)
  S=LBFGS_LOG_S
  if S<=0: zeros
  mean=float(np.dot(a,ga)/S)
  gy=a*(ga-mean)
  if not finite: zeros
  return gy
```

maybe `1 + sqrt(2) cos(π t)`? Let's test our high-frequency sinusoid idea more carefully with two-valued square wave. For f(t)=1+ρ cos(π m t), m odd, high m approximation gave peaks near x=1 where cos=+1 at distance 1/m, value ~ (1-1/m)(1+ρ^2/2). To keep max 0.75, choose ρ such that (1)(1+ρ^2/2)=0.75 impossible since >1. But the high-freq approximation includes L_x near1; peaks at distance 1/m are still high. Unless the triangle L_x not constant but includes oscillatory linear term O(1/m) that can subtract? For cos with m large, at x=1+1/m, L=1-1/m. hf contribution? cos(πm x)=cos(πm+πm/m)=cos(πm+π)=1 (since m odd cosπm=-1, -1* -? Let's compute m odd, cos(πm(1+1/m))=cos(πm+π)=cos(π(m+1))=cos evenπ? m+1 even =>1. Term=(ρ^2/2)L*1. So value~L(1+ρ^2/2)>0.75 for any ρ>0. So sine/cos nonnegative with positive variance cannot achieve below1. Need different phase in different intervals to cancel peaks, not simple trig.

What about f(t) = 1 + g(t) where g oscillates with zero mean and such that convolution high-frequency term is negative wherever L large. We need hf term ≈ -L^γ? The high-frequency product's second term is (1/2)cos(m x) L(x) for single frequency. To make negative across [1-δ,1+δ], need cos sign negative in that interval. But cos has zeros and positive peaks within interval if m large. Could choose m small? For cos(3πx) negative at x=1? m=3, cos(3π(4/3))=cos4π=1 at 1.333, away. Near x=1, positive at x=1±1/3? Max L at 1, if positive peaks at distance 1/3, L=2/3, value (2/3)(1+ρ^2/2). With ρ=1 max 1, not enough. Need designed phases.

Maybe optimal f is a fractal whose convolution is bounded by 3/4. Hard to explicit.

For single k, the minimizer of c_k with sum fixed and nonneg is equal split? c_k is ∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j. With variables in a linear combination of pairs. For a single k, minimizing c_k subject sum of involved variables fixed (and others fixed) is achieved by distributing mass equally among involved variables? If variables appear in multiple pairs, it's a quadratic. We could project onto equality ∑_{i in support_k} a_i = constant to minimize c_k. But multiple lags.

Our multiplicative update approximates this.

FFT_SIZE_CACHE = {}
ETA_GRID = (0.03, 0.08, 0.18, 0.35, 0.7, 1.3)

get_fft_size:
```
def get_fft_size(length):
    key=int(length)
    if key not in FFT_SIZE_CACHE:
        s=1
        while s < key:
            s <<= 1
        FFT_SIZE_CACHE[key]=s
    return s
```

fast_conv:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n==1:
        return np.array([a[0]*a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    m=2*n-1
    N=get_fft_size(m)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)
    return c[:m]
```

fast_cross similar.

weighted_lag_gradient:
```
def weighted_lag_gradient(a,w):
   a=np.asarray...; w=np...
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.array([])
   conv=fast_cross(a[::-1], w)
   return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
For n=1 slice 0:1 okay.

normalize_sum:
```
def normalize_sum(a,S):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy().ravel()
   if a.size==0:
       return a
   if S <=0: S=1.0
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=1e-12:
       a.fill(S/a.size); return a
   a = a * (S/s)
   a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
   s2=float(a.sum())
   if s2 <=1e-12:
       a.fill(S/a.size)
   else:
       a *= S/s2
   return a
```
If S large? S~118, clip no effect. Good.

loga_to_a:
```
def loga_to_a(loga,S):
   loga=np.asarray(loga,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if loga.size==0: return np.array([])
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(loga)):
       a=np.ones(loga.size)* (S/loga.size); return a
   mx=np.max(loga)
   x=np.clip(loga - mx, -60.0, 60.0)
   a=np.exp(x)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       a=np.ones(loga.size)
   else:
       a *= S/s
   a=np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
   s2=float(a.sum())
   if s2 <=1e-12:
      a=np.ones(loga.size)*(S/loga.size)
   else:
      a *= S/s2
   return a
```
If S=0? set 1.

make_loga:

def make_loga(a):
   a=np.asarray(a...); return np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15))
```

score_and_conv:
```
def score_and_conv(a):
   a=np.asarray...
   n=a.size
   S=float(a.sum())
   if n==0 or S<=1e-12:
      return np.inf, np.zeros(2*n-1 if n else 0), 0.0
   c=fast_conv(a)
   M=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   sc=2.0*n*M/(S*S)
   return sc,c,M
```

weights functions. Need handle `frac=0`? not.
```
def weights_top(c, frac, eps=1e-4):
    c=np.asarray(c,float); L=c.size
    if L==0: return np.array([])
    if frac >= 1.0:
       w=np.ones(L); w/=L
    else:
       k=int(round(L*frac))
       k=max(1,min(k,L))
       if k >= L: w=np.ones(L); w/=L
       else:
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
          w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0; w/=w.sum()
    if eps>0:
       w=(1.0-eps)*w + eps/L
       w/=w.sum()
    return w
```
If c has NaN? should not. If argpartition with -k when k=0 no.

weights_threshold:
```
def weights_threshold(c,M,delta,power=1,eps=1e-5):
   L=c.size
   if L==0: return np.array([])
   if M <= 1e-30:
      w=np.ones(L)/L
   else:
      thresh = M * max(0.0, 1.0 - delta)
      pos = c - thresh
      pos = np.where(pos > 0.0, pos, 0.0)
      ps = float(pos.sum())
      if ps <= 1e-15:
         w=np.zeros(L)
         idx=np.flatnonzero(c >= thresh - 1e-12*max(1.0,M))
         if idx.size ==0:
             idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
         w[idx]=1.0; w/=w.sum()
      else:
         w=pos
         if power != 1.0:
             w=np.power(w, power)
         ws=float(w.sum())
         if ws <=1e-15:
             w=np.ones(L); w/=L
         else:
             w/=ws
   if eps>0:
       w=(1-eps)*w + eps/L
       w/=w.sum()
   return w
```
Power as float; if power=1 skip.

weights_softmax:
```
def weights_softmax(c,M,beta,eps=1e-5):
  L=c.size
  if L==0: return np.array([])
  if M<=1e-30:
     w=np.ones(L)/L
  else:
     scaled=beta*(c-M)/M
     scaled=np.minimum(scaled,0.0)
     w=np.exp(scaled)
     ws=float(w.sum())
     if ws<=1e-300:
        w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
     else: w/=ws
  if eps>0: w=(1-eps)*w + eps/L; w/=w.sum()
  return w
```

get_weights:
```
def get_weights(c,M,config):
  typ=config[0]
  if typ=='top': return weights_top(c, config[1])
  elif typ=='thr': return weights_threshold(c,M,config[1],config[2])
  elif typ=='soft': return weights_softmax(c,M,config[1])
  else: return weights_top(c,0.01)
```

build_configs:
```
def build_weight_configs(rng):
  configs=[]
  for frac in (0.0005,0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35): configs.append(('top',frac))
  for delta in (0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
     configs.append(('thr',delta,1.0))
     if delta <=0.1:
        configs.append(('thr',delta,2.0))
  for beta in (0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0,10.0,20.0,50.0,100.0,300.0,1000.0):
     configs.append(('soft',beta))
  if rng is not None: rng.shuffle(configs)
  # ensure a mix at start? shuffle okay.
  return configs
```
Will a tuple containing floats; rng.shuffle list of tuples okay.

random_perturb functions:
```
def random_log_perturb(a, rng, strength):
   loga=make_loga(a)
   loga = loga + strength * rng.standard_normal(a.size)
   return loga_to_a(loga, a.sum())

def smooth_multiplicative_perturb(a, rng, amp):
   n=a.size
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   f1=1.0 + amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*(1.0+8.0*rng.random())*x + rng.random()*2.0*np.pi)
   f2=1.0 + 0.5*amp*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*(2.0+8.0*rng.random())*x + rng.random()*2.0*np.pi)
   a2=a*f1*f2
   # small smoothing
   if n >= 5:
      kernel=np.array([1.0,2.0,3.0,2.0,1.0])/9.0
      a2=np.convolve(a2, kernel, mode='same')
   a2=np.maximum(a2,1e-15)
   return normalize_sum(a2, a.sum())

def endpoint_taper(a, r):
   a2=a.astype(np.float64).copy()
   if a2.size==0: return a2
   S=a2.sum()
   a2[0]*=r
   a2[-1]*=r
   if a2.size>2:
      sr=np.sqrt(r)
      a2[1]*=sr
      a2[-2]*=sr
   a2=np.maximum(a2,1e-15)
   return normalize_sum(a2,S)

def generate_perturbations(a,rng):
   out=[]
   n=a.size
   for s in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2):
      out.append(random_log_perturb(a,rng,s))
   for amp in (0.04,0.08):
      out.append(smooth_multiplicative_perturb(a,rng,amp))
   for r in (0.75,0.9):
      out.append(endpoint_taper(a,r))
   # top red
   top_count=max(3, int(n//500))
   for red in (0.5,0.8):
      ar=a.astype(np.float64).copy()
      if ar.size>1:
         idx=np.argsort(ar)[::-1][:top_count]
         ar[idx]*=red
      ar=np.maximum(ar,1e-15)
      out.append(normalize_sum(ar, a.sum()))
   return out
```

multiplicative_refine:
Need use `score_and_conv` many. To reduce repeated exact_score in trial, call `exact_score` (score_and_conv). But for trial, we only need score; use exact_score to avoid c memory? It still allocates. okay.

```
def multiplicative_refine(a0, max_wall_s, rng, tag=''):
    a0=np.asarray(a0,float).ravel()
    if a0.size < 2:
       a0=np.ones(max(8,a0.size))*0.5
    n=a0.size
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_sum(a0,S)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-15); a*=S/a.sum()
    loga=make_loga(a)
    sc,c,M=score_and_conv(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
       a=np.ones(n)*(S/n); sc,c,M=score_and_conv(a)
    best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); best_loga=loga.copy()
    cur_a=a.copy(); cur_loga=loga.copy(); cur_sc=sc
    last_print=sc
    configs=build_weight_configs(rng)
    cfg_idx=0
    no_accept=0
    t_start=time.monotonic(); end=t_start+max(0.5,float(max_wall_s))
    print(f"[{tag}] refine start score={sc:.9f} n={n} wall={max_wall_s:.0f}s")
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       if cur_sc > best_sc + 1e-4:
           cur_a=best_a.copy(); cur_loga=best_loga.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
       sc,c,M=score_and_conv(cur_a)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=cur_a.copy(); best_loga=cur_loga.copy()
           if sc < last_print - 1e-5:
               print(f"[{tag}] new best {sc:.9f} n={n} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.0f}s"); last_print=sc
       accepted=False
       # try up to 8 configs
       for _ in range(8):
           if time.monotonic() >= end: break
           cfg=configs[cfg_idx % len(configs)]; cfg_idx += 1
           w=get_weights(c,M,cfg)
           if w is None or w.size != c.size: continue
           h=weighted_lag_gradient(cur_a,w)
           if h.size != n: continue
           mu=float(np.dot(cur_a,h)/S)
           d=mu-h
           sigma=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d)))
           if not np.isfinite(sigma) or sigma <=1e-15:
               continue
           d=np.clip(d/sigma, -5.0, 5.0)
           best_trial_sc=cur_sc
           best_trial_a=cur_a
           best_trial_loga=cur_loga
           improved=False
           for eta in ETA_GRID:
               logt=cur_loga + eta*d
               logt = np.clip(logt - np.max(logt), -60.0, 60.0)
               at=loga_to_a(logt,S)
               if at.size != n: continue
               st=exact_score(at)
               if np.isfinite(st) and st < best_trial_sc:
                   best_trial_sc=st; best_trial_a=at.copy(); best_trial_loga=logt.copy(); improved=True
           if improved:
               cur_a=best_trial_a; cur_loga=best_trial_loga.copy(); cur_sc=best_trial_sc
               accepted=True
               no_accept=0
               break
           # accept near flat to break tie? maybe if improved flag false but best_trial_sc < cur_sc -1e-12? that's improved. If exactly equal no.
       if not accepted:
           no_accept += 1
           if no_accept >= 4:
               # explore
               best_perturb=best_sc
               chosen_a=None
               for ap in generate_perturbations(best_a, rng):
                   sp=exact_score(ap)
                   if np.isfinite(sp) and sp < best_perturb:
                       best_perturb=sp; chosen_a=ap
               if chosen_a is not None and best_perturb < best_sc:
                   cur_a=chosen_a; cur_loga=make_loga(cur_a); cur_loga=np.clip(cur_loga - max(cur_loga), -60,60); cur_sc=best_perturb
               else:
                   # pick a perturbation not too bad
                   ap = random_log_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.1)
                   sp=exact_score(ap)
                   if sp < best_sc + 0.05:
                       cur_a=ap; cur_sc=sp; cur_loga=np.clip(make_loga(cur_a)-np.max(cur_loga),-60,60)
                   else:
                       cur_a=best_a.copy(); cur_loga=best_loga.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
               no_accept=0
               configs=build_weight_configs(rng)
    if best_sc < last_print - 1e-5:
       print...
    print(f"[{tag}] refine end best={best_sc:.9f}")
    return best_sc,best_a,best_loga
```

Now, L-BFGS log functions.
```
def lse_a_grad(a, beta): ...
def lbfgs_lse_log_fun(x):
   if x.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e10
   a=loga_to_a(x, LBFGS_LOG_S)
   return lse_a_grad(a, LSE_BETA)[0]
def lbfgs_lse_log_jac(x):
   if x.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return np.zeros(x.size)
   a=loga_to_a(x, LBFGS_LOG_S)
   sc,ga=lse_a_grad(a,LSE_BETA)
   S=LBFGS_LOG_S
   if S<=1e-12: return np.zeros(x.size)
   mean=float(np.dot(a,ga)/S)
   gy=a*(ga-mean)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)): gy=np.zeros(x.size)
   return gy
```
In `run_lbfgs_log`, set global.
```
def run_lbfgs_log(a0,beta,maxiter=800,maxfun=2000):
    global LSE_BETA, LBFGS_LOG_S
    a0=np.asarray(a0,float).ravel()
    n=a0.size
    if n==0: return np.inf, np.array([])
    S=float(a0.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12: S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    LSE_BETA=float(beta); LBFGS_LOG_S=float(S)
    y0=make_loga(a0)
    y0 = y0 - np.max(y0)
    options={'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':40}
    try:
       res=minimize(lbfgs_lse_log_fun, y0, jac=lbfgs_lse_log_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', options=options)
       yout=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
    except Exception:
       yout=y0
    aout=loga_to_a(yout,S)
    aout=np.clip(aout,0.0,1000.0)
    aout=normalize_sum(aout,S)
    sc=exact_score(aout)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=exact_score(a0)
    return sc,aout
```
Maybe `np.all(np.isfinite(x))` in jac called many times; okay. Could optimize by just `if not np.isfinite(np.max(x))`.

Now, resample:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size == n: return a.copy()
    if a.size ==0 or n==0: return np.zeros(n)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```

make_initial_sequence:
```
def make_initial_sequence(n,parent,rng,noise=0.01):
   if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
       a=resample_sequence(parent,n)
   else:
       x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       eps=0.002
       t1=np.power(x+eps, -0.45) * np.exp(-0.2*x)
       t2=np.power(np.sin(0.5*np.pi*x), 1.5)
       t3=np.power(np.cos(0.5*np.pi*x), 1.5)
       t4=0.5 + 0.4*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*(2.0+rng.random())*x + rng.random()*2.0*np.pi)
       t5=0.5 + 0.4*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*(5.0+rng.random()*5.0)*x + rng.random()*2.0*np.pi)
       a=0.35*t1+0.2*t2+0.2*t3+0.15*np.maximum(t4,0.0)+0.1*np.maximum(t5,0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-8)
   if noise>0 and a.size>1:
       a = a * np.exp(noise * rng.standard_normal(a.size))
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
   return normalize_sum(a, np.sqrt(2.0*float(a.size)))
```
For parent, noise might alter; primary noise=0. But if parent has zeros, no noise floor. Good.

`consider candidate` not needed.

def final_polish(a, max_wall_s, rng):
    t_start=time.monotonic(); end=t_start+max(0.5,float(max_wall_s))

    return best_sc,best_a

   alt_len = 8192
   alt = resample_sequence(best_a, alt_len); alt=normalize_sum...
   scale and list; verified2=evaluate; if finite return alt else original.
```
But re-evaluating another large sequence adds time. Only if inf; rare.

Now, let's double-check exact LSE formula in `lse_a_grad`: h = weighted_lag_gradient(a,p). p sum1. score=2nL/S^2. grad_a = 4n(h/S^2 - L a/S^3). Let's verify derivative of L wrt a: derivative L = 2h. derivative score = 2n*( (2h S^2 - L*2S*δS?)/S^4 ). If no sum constraint: =4n h/S^2 -4n L a/S^3. Yes. Good.
In log gradient, mean with S. Good.

One subtlety: p in LSE should sum to 1? In LSE derivative L = ∑ p ∂c where p=exp/sum, sum1. h computed with p sum1. Yes.

Now, in multiplicative weights, w sum1; F=∑w c; grad=2h. Our log steepest direction uses h not 2h; scaling by 2 irrelevant. mu=dot(a,h)/S. good.

Now, possible issue: In multiplicative trial, exact_score uses full score 2nM/S^2. Since S fixed, proportional to M. Good. If normalize_sum after a_trial clips to 1000 (not), renormalizes. Good.

Let's consider if current a has some entries exactly 0 (floor 1e-15). h may be small. mu positive. d positive for zero entries; log update increases them. Good.

Now, let's write final code with correct indentation. Need ensure no `lambda` anywhere. I won't use lambda. f-strings okay.

Let's produce final.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Method:
- Keep the sequence normalized to sum sqrt(2n), so the score is essentially the
  peak of the linear self-convolution.
- Use an exponentiated-gradient / multiplicative local search driven by
  dynamically chosen weights over the near-peak convolution lags.
- Add targeted structured perturbations to escape local basins.
- Use a log-domain L-BFGS-B polish with an LSE-smoothed objective.
- Explore nearby lengths and a coarsen-resample-refine path.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

FFT_SIZE_CACHE = {}
ETA_GRID = (0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.18, 0.35, 0.7, 1.3)

LSE_BETA = 100.0
LBFGS_LOG_S = 1.0


def get_fft_size(length):
    """Return the next power of two at least as large as length."""
    key = int(length)
    if key not in FFT_SIZE_CACHE:
        s = 1
        while s < key:
            s <<= 1
        FFT_SIZE_CACHE[key] = s
    return s


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = get_fft_size(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)
    return c[:m].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def fast_cross(a, b):
    """Linear cross-convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    na, nb = a.size, b.size
    if na == 0 or nb == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = na + nb - 1
    nfft = get_fft_size(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, nfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * B, nfft)[:m].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def weighted_lag_gradient(a, w):
    """
    For w indexed by convolution lag k, return h_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}.
    This is the unnormalized gradient of sum_k w_k (a*a)_k.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    conv = fast_cross(a[::-1], w)
    return conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def normalize_sum(a, S):
    """Clip to non-negative, finite range, and renormalize to exact sum S."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        S = 1.0

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a.fill(S / a.size)
        return a
    a *= S / s

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a.fill(S / a.size)
    else:
        a *= S / s
    return a


def make_loga(a):
    """Logarithm of a positive version of a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    return np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-15)).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def loga_to_a(loga, S):
    """Convert log-heights back to a non-negative vector with exact sum S."""
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if loga.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        S = 1.0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(loga)):
        return np.full(loga.size, S / loga.size, dtype=np.float64)

    mx = float(np.max(loga))
    x = np.clip(loga - mx, -60.0, 60.0)
    a = np.exp(x)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(loga.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a *= S / s

    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a.fill(S / a.size)
    else:
        a *= S / s
    return a.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def score_and_conv(a):
    """Return (score, convolution, peak convolution) for a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    c = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    sc = 2.0 * n * M / (S * S)
    return sc, c, M


def exact_score(a):
    """Exact score for a."""
    return score_and_conv(a)[0]


def weights_top(c, frac, eps=1e-4):
    """Uniform weights on the top frac lags, plus tiny uniform floor."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if frac >= 1.0:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= L
    else:
        k = int(round(float(L) * float(frac)))
        k = max(1, min(k, L))
        if k >= L:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w /= L
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0
            w /= max(1e-300, float(w.sum()))

    if eps > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - eps) * w + eps / float(L)
        w /= max(1e-300, float(w.sum()))
    return w


def weights_threshold(c, M, delta, power=1.0, eps=1e-5):
    """Weights proportional to (c - (M*(1-delta)))^power on positive part."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-30:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
    else:
        thresh = M * max(0.0, 1.0 - float(delta))
        pos = c - thresh
        pos = np.where(pos > 0.0, pos, 0.0)
        ps = float(pos.sum())
        if ps <= 1e-15:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(M))
            idx = np.flatnonzero(c >= thresh - tol)
            if idx.size == 0:
                idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
            w[idx] = 1.0
            w /= max(1e-300, float(w.sum()))
        else:
            w = pos.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
            if abs(float(power) - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                w = np.power(w, float(power))
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 1e-15:
                w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
            else:
                w /= ws

    if eps > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - eps) * w + eps / float(L)
        w /= max(1e-300, float(w.sum()))
    return w


def weights_softmax(c, M, beta, eps=1e-5):
    """Softmax weights on (c - M), scaled by beta/M."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-30:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
    else:
        scaled = float(beta) * (c - M) / M
        scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(scaled)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-300:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= ws

    if eps > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - eps) * w + eps / float(L)
        w /= max(1e-300, float(w.sum()))
    return w


def get_weights(c, M, config):
    """Build weights from a config tuple."""
    typ = config[0]
    if typ == "top":
        return weights_top(c, config[1])
    if typ == "thr":
        return weights_threshold(c, M, config[1], config[2])
    if typ == "soft":
        return weights_softmax(c, M, config[1])
    return weights_top(c, 0.01)


def build_weight_configs(rng):
    """A shuffled list of weight configurations for local search."""
    configs = []

    for frac in (0.0005, 0.001, 0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35):
        configs.append(("top", float(frac)))

    for delta in (0.001, 0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4):
        delta = float(delta)
        configs.append(("thr", delta, 1.0))
        if delta <= 0.1:
            configs.append(("thr", delta, 2.0))

    for beta in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0):
        configs.append(("soft", float(beta)))

    if rng is not None:
        rng.shuffle(configs)
    return configs


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    """Resample a one-dimensional sequence to length n by linear interpolation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def random_log_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    """Multiplicative random perturbation in log-space."""
    loga = make_loga(a)
    loga = loga + float(strength) * rng.standard_normal(a.size)
    return loga_to_a(loga, a.sum())


def smooth_multiplicative_perturb(a, rng, amp):
    """Smooth low-frequency multiplicative perturbation."""
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    f1 = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(
        2.0 * np.pi * (1.0 + 8.0 * rng.random()) * x + rng.random() * 2.0 * np.pi
    )
    f2 = 1.0 + 0.5 * float(amp) * np.sin(
        2.0 * np.pi * (2.0 + 8.0 * rng.random()) * x + rng.random() * 2.0 * np.pi
    )
    a2 = a * f1 * f2
    if n >= 5:
        kernel = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 2.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        kernel /= 9.0
        a2 = np.convolve(a2, kernel, mode="same")
    a2 = np.maximum(a2, 1e-15)
    return normalize_sum(a2, a.sum())


def endpoint_taper(a, r):
    """Reduce endpoints to relieve boundary square peaks."""
    a2 = a.astype(np.float64).copy()
    if a2.size == 0:
        return a2
    S = float(a2.sum())
    rr = float(r)
    a2[0] *= rr
    if a2.size > 1:
        a2[-1] *= rr
    if a2.size > 2:
        sr = np.sqrt(rr)
        a2[1] *= sr
        a2[-2] *= sr
    a2 = np.maximum(a2, 1e-15)
    return normalize_sum(a2, S)


def generate_perturbations(a, rng):
    """A small structured set of perturbations of a."""
    out = []
    n = a.size

    for s in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2):
        out.append(random_log_perturb(a, rng, s))

    for amp in (0.04, 0.08):
        out.append(smooth_multiplicative_perturb(a, rng, amp))

    for r in (0.75, 0.9):
        out.append(endpoint_taper(a, r))

    if n > 1:
        top_count = min(n, max(3, n // 500))
        for red in (0.5, 0.8):
            ar = a.astype(np.float64).copy()
            idx = np.argsort(ar)[::-1][:top_count]
            ar[idx] *= float(red)
            ar = np.maximum(ar, 1e-15)
            out.append(normalize_sum(ar, a.sum()))

    return out


def multiplicative_refine(a0, max_wall_s, rng, tag=""):
    """
    Local search by exponentiated gradient steps on weighted near-max lags.
    Returns (best_score, best_a, best_loga).
    """
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a0.size < 2:
        a0 = np.ones(max(8, a0.size), dtype=np.float64) * 0.5

    n = a0.size
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))

    a = normalize_sum(a0, S)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    a *= S / max(1e-300, float(a.sum()))
    loga = make_loga(a)

    sc, c, M = score_and_conv(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        a = np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
        loga = make_loga(a)
        sc, c, M = score_and_conv(a)

    best_sc = float(sc)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_loga = loga.copy()

    cur_a = a.copy()
    cur_loga = loga.copy()
    cur_sc = float(sc)

    configs = build_weight_configs(rng)
    cfg_idx = 0
    no_accept = 0
    last_print = best_sc

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    end = t_start + max(0.5, float(max_wall_s))
    print(f"[{tag}] multiplicative start score={best_sc:.9f} n={n} wall={max_wall_s:.0f}s")

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        if cur_sc > best_sc + 1e-4:
            cur_a = best_a.copy()
            cur_loga = best_loga.copy()
            cur_sc = best_sc

        sc, c, M = score_and_conv(cur_a)
        if (not np.isfinite(sc)) or (not np.isfinite(M)):
            cur_a = best_a.copy()
            cur_loga = best_loga.copy()
            cur_sc = best_sc
            continue

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = cur_a.copy()
            best_loga = cur_loga.copy()
            if best_sc < last_print - 1e-5:
                print(f"[{tag}] new best {best_sc:.9f} n={n} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.0f}s")
                last_print = best_sc

        accepted = False

        for _ in range(8):
            if time.monotonic() >= end:
                break

            cfg = configs[cfg_idx % len(configs)]
            cfg_idx += 1

            w = get_weights(c, M, cfg)
            if w is None or w.size != c.size:
                continue

            h = weighted_lag_gradient(cur_a, w)
            if h.size != n:
                continue

            mu = float(np.dot(cur_a, h) / S)
            d = mu - h
            sigma = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
            if (not np.isfinite(sigma)) or sigma <= 1e-15:
                continue

            d = np.clip(d / sigma, -4.0, 4.0)

            best_trial_sc = cur_sc
            best_trial_a = cur_a
            best_trial_loga = cur_loga
            improved = False

            for eta in ETA_GRID:
                logt = cur_loga + float(eta) * d
                logt = np.clip(logt - float(np.max(logt)), -60.0, 60.0)
                at = loga_to_a(logt, S)
                if at.size != n:
                    continue
                st = exact_score(at)
                if np.isfinite(st) and st < best_trial_sc:
                    best_trial_sc = float(st)
                    best_trial_a = at.copy()
                    best_trial_loga = make_loga(at)
                    improved = True

            if improved:
                cur_a = best_trial_a
                cur_loga = best_trial_loga
                cur_sc = best_trial_sc
                accepted = True
                no_accept = 0
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept >= 4:
                chosen_a = None
                chosen_sc = best_sc

                for ap in generate_perturbations(best_a, rng):
                    sp = exact_score(ap)
                    if np.isfinite(sp) and sp < chosen_sc:
                        chosen_sc = float(sp)
                        chosen_a = ap

                if chosen_a is not None and chosen_sc < best_sc:
                    cur_a = chosen_a
                    cur_sc = chosen_sc
                    cur_loga = make_loga(cur_a)
                    cur_loga = np.clip(cur_loga - float(np.max(cur_loga)), -60.0, 60.0)
                else:
                    ap = random_log_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.1)
                    sp = exact_score(ap)
                    if np.isfinite(sp) and sp < best_sc + 0.05:
                        cur_a = ap
                        cur_sc = float(sp)
                        cur_loga = make_loga(cur_a)
                        cur_loga = np.clip(cur_loga - float(np.max(cur_loga)), -60.0, 60.0)
                    else:
                        cur_a = best_a.copy()
                        cur_sc = best_sc
                        cur_loga = best_loga.copy()

                no_accept = 0
                configs = build_weight_configs(rng)

    if best_sc < last_print - 1e-5:
        print(f"[{tag}] multiplicative final best {best_sc:.9f} n={n}")
    return best_sc, best_a, best_loga


def candidate_score_key(item):
    """Sort key for candidate tuples (score, array, label)."""
    return item[0]


def lse_a_grad(a, beta):
    """LSE-smoothed score and Euclidean gradient with respect to a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    S = float(a.sum())
    if n == 0 or S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    scaled = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    es = float(e.sum())

    if es <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = e / es

    L = cmax + float(np.log(max(1e-300, es))) / float(beta)
    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S

    h = weighted_lag_gradient(a, p)
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * L / S2
    grad_a = 4.0 * float(n) * (h / S2 - L * a / S3)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_a)):
        score = 1e10
        grad_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return score, grad_a


def lbfgs_lse_log_fun(x):
    """Objective for log-domain L-BFGS."""
    if x.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e10
    a = loga_to_a(x, LBFGS_LOG_S)
    sc, _ = lse_a_grad(a, LSE_BETA)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e10
    return float(sc)


def lbfgs_lse_log_jac(x):
    """Jacobian for log-domain L-BFGS."""
    if x.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)

    a = loga_to_a(x, LBFGS_LOG_S)
    _, ga = lse_a_grad(a, LSE_BETA)
    S = LBFGS_LOG_S
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)

    mean_g = float(np.dot(a, ga) / S)
    gy = a * (ga - mean_g)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)):
        gy = np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return gy


def run_lbfgs_log(a0, beta, maxiter=800, maxfun=2000):
    """
    Log-domain L-BFGS-B polish using LSE-smoothed objective.
    The sum is fixed inside the log parameterization.
    """
    global LSE_BETA, LBFGS_LOG_S

    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    S = float(a0.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))

    LSE_BETA = float(beta)
    LBFGS_LOG_S = float(S)

    y0 = make_loga(a0)
    y0 = y0 - float(np.max(y0))

    options = {
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 40,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lbfgs_lse_log_fun,
            y0,
            jac=lbfgs_lse_log_jac,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options=options,
        )
        yout = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        yout = y0

    aout = loga_to_a(yout, S)
    aout = np.clip(aout, 0.0, 1000.0)
    aout = normalize_sum(aout, S)
    sc = exact_score(aout)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = exact_score(a0)
    return float(sc), aout


def make_initial_sequence(n, parent, rng, noise=0.01):
    """Create a positive normalized starting sequence of length n."""
    n = int(n)
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        a = resample_sequence(parent, n)
    else:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        eps = 0.002
        t1 = np.power(x + eps, -0.45) * np.exp(-0.2 * x)
        t2 = np.power(np.sin(0.5 * np.pi * x), 1.5)
        t3 = np.power(np.cos(0.5 * np.pi * x), 1.5)
        t4 = 0.5 + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (2.0 + rng.random()) * x + rng.random() * 2.0 * np.pi)
        t5 = 0.5 + 0.4 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (5.0 + 5.0 * rng.random()) * x + rng.random() * 2.0 * np.pi)
        a = (
            0.35 * t1
            + 0.20 * t2
            + 0.20 * t3
            + 0.15 * np.maximum(t4, 0.0)
            + 0.10 * np.maximum(t5, 0.0)
        )

    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    if noise > 0.0 and a.size > 1:
        a = a * np.exp(float(noise) * rng.standard_normal(a.size))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return normalize_sum(a, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(a.size)))


def final_polish(a, max_wall_s, rng):
    """Final polish: multiplicative steps plus log-domain L-BFGS."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    end = t_start + max(0.5, float(max_wall_s))

    a = normalize_sum(a, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(a.size)))
    sc = exact_score(a)
    best_sc = float(sc)
    best_a = a.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < end - 60.0:
        alloc = min(300.0, 0.35 * (end - time.monotonic()))
        if alloc > 10.0:
            sc, a, _ = multiplicative_refine(a, alloc, rng, "final_mul1")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < end - 40.0:
        n = best_a.size
        for beta in (50.0, 200.0, 1000.0):
            if time.monotonic() >= end - 30.0:
                break
            maxfun = 2000 if n <= 8000 else 1200
            sc2, a2 = run_lbfgs_log(best_a, beta, maxiter=800, maxfun=maxfun)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc2)
                best_a = a2.copy()
                print(f"[final_lbfgs beta={beta:.0f}] new best {best_sc:.9f} n={n}")

            if time.monotonic() < end - 60.0:
                scm, am, _ = multiplicative_refine(best_a, 30.0, rng, "post_lbfgs")
                if np.isfinite(scm) and scm < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(scm)
                    best_a = am.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < end - 5.0:
        alloc = end - time.monotonic() - 2.0
        sc, a, _ = multiplicative_refine(best_a, max(1.0, alloc), rng, "final_mul2")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_sc, best_a


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, seed=7, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best sequence within budget_s seconds.
    Returns a Python list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(120.0, total_budget - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0:
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        base_len = int(parent.size)
    else:
        base_len = 6962
    base_len = int(max(2048, min(base_len, 12288)))

    raw_lengths = {base_len}
    for dl in (-1024, 1024, 2048):
        L = base_len + dl
        if 2048 <= L <= 12288:
            raw_lengths.add(L)
    for L in (8192, 10240, 12288):
        if 2048 <= L <= 12288:
            raw_lengths.add(L)
    lengths = sorted(raw_lengths)[:5]

    candidates = []
    best_score = np.inf
    best_a = np.ones(base_len, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    primary_label = None

    # Base parent/template at parent length, no noise.
    a_base = make_initial_sequence(base_len, parent, rng, 0.0)
    sc_base = exact_score(a_base)
    candidates.append((float(sc_base), a_base, "base"))
    if sc_base < best_score:
        best_score = float(sc_base)
        best_a = a_base.copy()
        primary_label = "base"

    # Nearby lengths and small randomized variants.
    for L in lengths:
        for noise in (0.01, 0.03):
            label = f"L{L}_n{noise:.2f}"
            if label == "base":
                continue
            a_c = make_initial_sequence(int(L), parent, rng, float(noise))
            sc_c = exact_score(a_c)
            candidates.append((float(sc_c), a_c, label))
            if sc_c < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_c)
                best_a = a_c.copy()
                primary_label = label

    print(
        f"Initial best score={best_score:.9f}, primary_label={primary_label}, "
        f"parent=len{parent.size if parent is not None else 0}, lengths={lengths}"
    )

    # Main refinement of the best initial sequence.
    now = time.monotonic()
    primary_time = max(60.0, 0.55 * (deadline - now))
    if now + primary_time < deadline:
        sc, a, _ = multiplicative_refine(best_a, primary_time, rng, "primary")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()

    # Explore a few other initial lengths/bashes if time remains.
    candidates.sort(key=candidate_score_key)
    others = []
    for item in candidates:
        if item[2] != primary_label:
            others.append(item)
        if len(others) >= 4:
            break

    for item in others:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 500.0:
            break
        alloc = min(120.0, max(20.0, 0.2 * (deadline - now - 500.0)))
        if now + alloc >= deadline:
            alloc = max(10.0, deadline - now - 10.0)
        sc, a, _ = multiplicative_refine(item[1], alloc, rng, item[2])
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()
            print(f"[explore {item[2]}] new best {best_score:.9f} n={a.size}")

    # Coarsen, optimize coarsely, then upsample under current best length.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 700.0 and best_a.size > 512:
        n_best = best_a.size
        m_len = max(256, int(n_best // 8))
        a_coarse = resample_sequence(best_a, int(m_len))
        a_coarse = normalize_sum(a_coarse, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(a_coarse.size)))
        alloc1 = min(60.0, 0.5 * (deadline - now - 650.0))
        if alloc1 > 10.0:
            _, a_coarse, _ = multiplicative_refine(a_coarse, alloc1, rng, "coarse")

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 250.0:
            a_up = resample_sequence(a_coarse, int(n_best))
            a_up = normalize_sum(a_up, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n_best)))
            alloc2 = min(120.0, max(20.0, 0.5 * (deadline - time.monotonic() - 250.0)))
            if alloc2 > 10.0:
                sc_up, a_up, _ = multiplicative_refine(a_up, alloc2, rng, "coarse_up")
                if np.isfinite(sc_up) and sc_up < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc_up)
                    best_a = a_up.copy()
                    print(f"[coarse_up] new best {best_score:.9f} n={n_best}")

    # Final polish with remaining time.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 10.0:
        sc, a, _ = final_polish(best_a, max(1.0, deadline - now - 5.0), rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()

    # Prepare output: scale into [0, 1000], preserving the scale-invariant score.
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if out_arr.size == 0:
        out_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5

    out_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(out_arr), out_arr, 0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    mx = float(np.max(out_arr))
    if mx <= 1e-300:
        out_arr = np.ones(max(64, out_arr.size), dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr *= 999.0 / mx
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in out_arr]

    if (not out) or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    # Fallback if evaluation is invalid, e.g. because of an unexpected length issue.
    if not np.isfinite(verified) and best_a.size > 0:
        alt_len = 8192
        alt_arr = resample_sequence(best_a, int(alt_len))
        alt_arr = normalize_sum(alt_arr, np.sqrt(2.0 * float(alt_len)))
        alt_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(alt_arr), alt_arr, 0.0)
        alt_arr = np.clip(alt_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        amx = float(np.max(alt_arr))
        if amx > 1e-300:
            alt_arr *= 999.0 / amx
        alt_arr = np.clip(alt_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        alt_out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in alt_arr]
        try:
            verified_alt = float(evaluate_sequence(alt_out))
        except Exception:
            verified_alt = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(verified_alt):
            out = alt_out
            verified = verified_alt

    print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.9f}, verified={verified:.9f}, length={len(out)}, "
          f"time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    return out
```
